### 4.4 Map load mode: new game vs load

`FUN_0043ADF0(mode)` (`ev_9c1620dc0ac95e2e8dea2ee291a29d914ab0a795d98e5ea8deca3e9ce6a64dfd`)
loads `LIGHTS.<loc>` and `KONUNG.<loc>` and rebuilds the runtime world.

- **mode 0** (new game, from state 8): clears `Group+0x1D` for object/enemy
  groups (`+0x1E & 0x40`), places the initial population (`FUN_00411DE4`),
  spawns population growth, rebuilds the `0x785BB4` ground-marker list and
  re-projects WorldObjects.
- **mode 1** (load, from `FUN_004246C0`): skips the group-activity clear, does
  **not** re-place the population and does **not** rebuild `0x785BB4` /
  WorldObjects from `KONUNG.<loc>`; those come from the snapshot. It still

#### `drawer_konung_general_e6bd2d8d6c0603d53efe4659`

- **wing:** konung
- **room:** general
- **source_file:** C:\Users\z10ba\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\konung-docs-v6\save-load\KONUNG_SAVE_LOAD.md
- **filed_at:** 2026-10-21T20:29:46.998723
- **added_by:** opencode
- **hall:** general
- **chunk_index:** 18
- **chunk_total:** 22

ot** rebuild `0x785BB4` /
  WorldObjects from `KONUNG.<loc>`; those come from the snapshot. It still
  loads terrain/collision/GRAPH/props, activates types and refreshes entity
  anchors (`FUN_00414078`).

This is why the snapshot must carry `0x77DEB4`, `0x785BB4`, `0x78A250` and
`0x81D6A8`: mode 1 deliberately does not regenerate them.

## 5. Screen integration

The save/load screens are top-level states of `_DAT_0081D5AC` (see
`KONUNG_STATE_MACHINE.md`):

#### `drawer_konung_general_6a72188b3526b21f5f5d714b`

tion

The save/load screens are top-level states of `_DAT_0081D5AC` (see
`KONUNG_STATE_MACHINE.md`):

| state | screen | open | confirm | failure/back |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `4` | load | `FUN_004304F4(0)` draws 10 slots; `_DAT_0081D678` from hit test | `FUN_004246C0()`; success → state `0` | → state `7`, menu page `4` |
| `5` | save | `FUN_004304F4(1)`; name buffer `0x81CE34` for selected slot | copy `0x81CE34` → `KONUNG.SAV[slot]`; `FUN_00424B3C()`; → state `0` | — |

`FUN_004304F4` (`ev_e273464e81dbbc1b32be53d4937e8535fa7537a458d60fc989756f582ef86cbe`)
uses the per-resolution 10-row rectangle table at `0x46241C` (stride `0x20`,
indexed by `_DAT_0081D644 * 0x10`).

## 6. Verified and unknown

**Verified**

- `KONUNG.CFG` = 7 × u32, 28 bytes, same order read/write.
- Option labels: Прозрачность, Смена дня и ночи, Световые эффекты, Музыка,
  Тени, Звук (plus 640×480 / 800×600 and OK/Cancel).
- `KONUNG.SAV` = 10 × 50-byte CP866 slot names, 500 bytes.
- `KONUNG.SA0`..`SA9` = 0xB07A4 bytes, exact block order and sizes.
- `0x80B0D0` = 100 × 0x20 projectile/effect pool; `_81D6A0` = active count.
- `0x81CF80` = 56 discovered world-map location flags.
- Save name = location default + space + `Www Mmm dd hh:mm` (asctime truncated).
- Load mode 1 does not regenerate population/WorldObjects/ground markers.
- Slot filename is `SA` + digit `0..9`; no other slot encoding exists.

# Konung — Startup, WinMain, message loop and thread model

Target: `konung.res` (PE32/i386, image base `0x400000`,
`sha256 d698c4f231489a372cc25212ab2819720a36d7ce5307a045db684135cf679810`).
All addresses are virtual addresses in `konung.res`.

This document covers the process entry chain, window/message setup, the 60 ms frame
tick, configuration load, shutdown, and the complete threading picture. The
screen state machine built on top of this loop is in `KONUNG_STATE_MACHINE.md`.

## 2. WinMain (`0x42B1A8`, stdcall, `ret 0x10`)

Reconstructed order of operations:

1. `_DAT_0081D5AC = -1` — invalidate the screen state before init.
2. Build `WNDCLASS` on the stack:
   - `style = 3` (`CS_HREDRAW | CS_VREDRAW`);
   - `lpfnWndProc = 0x42B65C`;
   - `cbClsExtra = cbWndExtra = 0`;
   - `hInstance = WinMain.hInstance`;
   - `hIcon = LoadIconA(hInstance, "GAME_ICON" @ 0x4502C2)`;
   - `hCursor = LoadCursorA(NULL, 0x7F00)` (`IDC_ARROW`);
   - `hbrBackground = GetStockObject(4)` (`BLACK_BRUSH`);
   - `lpszMenuName = NULL`, `lpszClassName = "Konung" @ 0x4632BC`.
3. `RegisterClassA(&wc)`.
4. `CreateWindowExA(8, "Konung", "Konung", 0x80000000, 0, 0,
   GetSystemMetrics(0), GetSystemMetrics(1), 0, 0, hInstance, 0)`.

### 2.1 Message/frame loop (`0x42B58C`–`0x42B647`)

```text
while (_DAT_0082D2C9) {
    if (_DAT_0081D5F4 != 0) {                 // movie mode: blocking pump
        if (GetMessageA(&msg,0,0,0)) {
            TranslateMessage(&msg); DispatchMessageA(&msg);
        }
    } else {                                  // normal mode: non-blocking pump
        if (PeekMessageA(&msg,0,0,0,PM_REMOVE)) {
            TranslateMessage(&msg); DispatchMessageA(&msg);
        } else if (_DAT_0082D2C8) {           // app active
            now = timeGetTime();
            if (now - last_tick >= _DAT_0081D5C8) {  // 60 ms
                last_tick = now;
                SendMessageA(hwnd, 0x0400, 0, 0);    // WM_USER: one frame
            }
        }

#### `drawer_konung_general_2bec7196394327aaf9deff82`

;
                SendMessageA(hwnd, 0x0400, 0, 0);    // WM_USER: one frame
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Consequences:

- The game is **event-driven**: one `WM_USER 0x400` frame is sent only when the
  app is active and 60 ms have elapsed since the previous frame.
- There is no `Sleep`/`timeSetEvent`/timer thread; the limiter is
  `timeGetTime` in the main loop.
- Mouse/keyboard messages are still pumped between frames; they only mutate the
  input state/ring and are consumed on the next frame.
- `WM_PAINT` also renders a frame (`FUN_0042CBD0(-1)` + `FUN_0042A470`) when the
  screen state is 0.

## 3. WndProc (`0x42B65C`, stdcall, `ret 0x10`)

Ghidra defines no function here (`resolve_containing_procedure(0x42B65C)` →
`not_in_procedure`). The body was decoded as raw instructions. Argument layout:
`hwnd=[ebp+0x14]`, `msg=[ebp+0x18]`, `wParam=[ebp+0x1C]`, `lParam=[ebp+0x20]`.

### 3.1 Quit pre-check

At entry: if `_DAT_0081D618 != 0` (quit requested), clear it, clear
`_DAT_0082D2C9`, `DestroyWindow(hwnd)`, return 0.

## 4. Thread model

**The game itself starts no threads.**

- The only `CreateThread` reference in the executable is the CRT helper
  `FUN_00444D1F` (`CreateThread` thunk `0x445CC2`,
  `ev_7380d8a430073023e62ce27ba60918b43a9f0f4ae707c382d3b5d3a79fda3b1c`).
  It creates a thread at `LAB_00444C6A` and waits on an event; it is the CRT
  `_beginthread`/`_beginthreadex` support routine
  (`ev_7343543e041534b6530da4bb42cb25074de3041f1dc4c6af7adfe3527e20c80c`).
- `FUN_00444D1F` has **no callers and no data references**
  (`procedure_callers` empty, `xrefs(0x444D1F)` empty). It is linked CRT code,
  never invoked by the game.
- `ExitThread`, `GetCurrentThread`, `GetCurrentThreadId` are likewise only in

#### `drawer_konung_general_99be0ceef4afbf6220ad14ac`

- **wing:** konung
- **room:** general
- **source_file:** C:\Users\z10ba\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\konung-docs-v8\startup\KONUNG_STARTUP_MAIN.md
- **filed_at:** 2026-10-21T20:31:35.519465
- **added_by:** opencode
- **hall:** creative
- **chunk_index:** 15
- **chunk_total:** 20

r invoked by the game.
- `ExitThread`, `GetCurrentThread`, `GetCurrentThreadId` are likewise only in
  the CRT import/helper region (`0x445CBC`–`0x445D16`).
- The movie path uses the external `PlayAVI.dll` (`_playAVIFile@8`,
  `ev_60a4959cd094a1f57949417189a0c968c80f9c458229305b0875803da823c63d`).
  Follow-up triage of `PlayAVI.dll` (`sha256 0282620e…142c`, 316 416 B, 2296
  procedures) shows it **does create one helper thread**:
  `FUN_004281F0` installs a thread-local `WH_GETMESSAGE` hook
  (`SetWindowsHookExA(3, …)`) and calls `CreateThread` once
  (`0x405EC0` → `CreateThread`; caller `0x4281F0`) with start routine
  `0x428150`. The thread is guarded by module globals (`DAT_0044576C`), so it
  is singleton. Body reconstructed from raw bytes: it stores its thread id,

- **added_by:** opencode
- **hall:** memory
- **chunk_index:** 16
- **chunk_total:** 20

(`DAT_0044576C`), so it
  is singleton. Body reconstructed from raw bytes: it stores its thread id,
  then loops `GetMessageA`-style processing for the active video object
  (`[obj+0x48]`), calls the frame processor `0x429FE4`, and waits on an event
  with a **100 ms** timeout (`WaitForSingleObject`, result `0x102` =
  `WAIT_TIMEOUT` repeats). It is a video-frame pump, not a second game loop.
- The frame tick is a Win32 message (`WM_USER 0x400`) produced by
  `timeGetTime` in the main loop, not a timer or worker thread.

So the documented concurrency model is: **one game/UI thread** + **one
PlayAVI.dll helper thread** (created lazily on the first movie frame, with a
thread-local message hook).

hread** (created lazily on the first movie frame, with a
thread-local message hook).

## 5. Shutdown

- Menu item “exit” calls `FUN_0043BF5C("",0)` (`0x436CD9`): it sets
  `_DAT_0081D618 = 1`, shuts down graphics/sound interfaces and copies the
  message into `0x81CE68` (`ev_7788706670363b96fab775d9d89cb879acc7a04fa087cf4c5c204d0d3f3ec0d7`).
- The next WndProc call sees `_DAT_0081D618 != 0`, clears the running flag and
  calls `DestroyWindow`.
- `WM_DESTROY` shuts down sound (`FUN_0042987C`), DirectDraw (`FUN_00429744`)
  and posts `WM_QUIT`, ending the loop.

## 7. Residual unknowns

1. `FUN_0043BFE4` — identified only as a CRT startup helper; its exact CRT
   function name is not needed by the game and is UNKNOWN.
2. `_DAT_0081D5D8`, `_DAT_0081D5E0`, `_DAT_0081D614` — initialised here, roles
   not fully resolved (`_81D614` is the trade-toggle state per
   `KEY_INPUT_SYSTEM.md`).
3. `0x03B9` is resolved as `MM_MCINOTIFY`: the MCI music device sends it to the
   game window; `wParam == 1` (`MCI_NOTIFY_SUCCESSFUL`) calls
   `FUN_00429630` to resume/play music (`mciSendCommandA`).

# Konung — function inventory and CLIENT.EXE triage

Target: `konung.res` (PE32/i386, image base `0x400000`,
`sha256 d698c4f231489a372cc25212ab2819720a36d7ce5307a045db684135cf679810`).

Full machine-readable inventory: `evidence/konung_res_functions.csv`
(one row per procedure; columns `address,name,kind,region,role,evidence_id`).

## 1. Method and coverage

- Provider: REA / Ghidra 12.1.4, default auto-analysis, complete procedure list
  (`list_procedures`, evidence `ev_b5e39e88c2b09068bbbe374a4a2b9a297af606f5cbb3de61547621d299da513e`).
- Addresses are Ghidra virtual addresses in `konung.res`.
- 627 total entries:
  - **72 external imports** (`EXTERNAL:*`, Win32/DirectX/DSound/PlayAVI/WinMM);
  - **43 import thunks** in the stub region (`0x445Cxx`–`0x445Dxx`);

## 3. Unclassified game functions

The CSV marks **0 internal game functions** as `unclassified`. They are not
guessed. They are clustered mainly in:

- `0x41xxxx`–`0x42xxxx` — combat/AI/item/interface helpers;
- `0x43xxxx` below `0x43E000` — loaders, render helpers, quest script helpers.

To promote one, use the recorded evidence style:
`analyze_function` → `procedure_callers/callees` → `procedure_pseudo_code`,
then update the CSV role and add a section to the relevant document.

**Conclusion:** `CLIENT.EXE` is a **separate, earlier/alternate client build**
(most likely the multiplayer/client variant: `SERVERS.CFG`, `"Server failed"`,
`Konung_Client` window class). It shares the CRT/stub conventions and the same
class of game code, but every address from `konung.res` must be re-derived.
It has no save-slot system and no PlayAVI movie path. It is **not** used by the
remake and was not deep-dived.

# Konung — game state machine

Target: `konung.res` (PE32/i386, image base `0x400000`,
`sha256 d698c4f231489a372cc25212ab2819720a36d7ce5307a045db684135cf679810`).
All addresses are virtual addresses in `konung.res`.

This is the top-level behavioural model of the shipped game: application
screen states, their transitions, the per-frame sequence, input routing, and
the sub-machines that run inside gameplay. File formats and low-level input
details are in the companion documents listed at the end.

## 1. Two independent state variables

Do not conflate these:

## 1. Two independent state variables

Do not conflate these:

| variable | address | domain | meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `_DAT_0081D5AC` | `0x81D5AC` | **top-level screen / application state** | `0..9`; selects which per-frame update+render block runs |
| `_DAT_0081D610` | `0x81D610` | **interface mode inside gameplay** | `0` world, `1` dialogue, `2` quest log, `3` character, `5` **world map / travel** (56 discovered-location flags), `7` inventory detail |
| `_DAT_0081D6B8` | `0x81D6B8` | **menu page selector** | used by states `1`/`7` to choose the MENU page |
| `_DAT_0081D618` | `0x81D618` | quit request | set by `FUN_0043BF5C`; consumed by WndProc |
| `_DAT_0081D5F8` | `0x81D5F8` | “a game was running” | ESC/menu bookkeeping |

## 2. Top-level states

| id | name | update block | render / screen | entry | exit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `0` | **GAMEPLAY** | `0x43653F` | world + HUD + interface | resume/new game/load | game over, menu, map transition |
| `1` | **MAIN MENU** | `0x43681E` | MENU page `_81D6B8` | state `7` (first frame) | item select |
| `2` | **NEW HERO** | `0x436D22` | character-creation screen, rect table `0x4604DC` | menu item “new game” | confirm → `8`, back → `7/2` |
| `3` | **FATAL / QUIT** | `0x4377ED` | error message | resolution/map failure | quit |
| `4` | **LOAD GAME** | `0x437802` | 10 save slots (`0x46241C`) | menu item “load” | load → `0`, fail → `7/4` |

consumed by WndProc |
| `_DAT_0081D5F8` | `0x81D5F8` | “a game was running” | ESC/menu bookkeeping |
| `_DAT_0081D61C` | `0x81D61C` | modal/gate flags | bit 0 gates the gameplay input block; bits 1/2/3 are interface-open requests |

`_DAT_0081D5AC` is initialised to `-1` at WinMain entry (`0x42B1B4`) and set to
`9` immediately after window creation (`0x42B2B0`). Every frame,
`FUN_00436214` (`0x436214`) reads it and dispatches through the jump table at
`0x438048` (`ev_2dc51220a03d87ec13709069686b40bf4ad97442546845786a6b454607d03a04`).

## 3. State diagram

```mermaid
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IntroMovie: WinMain sets state 9
    IntroMovie --> MenuEntry: movie completes\n(_81D5AC = _81D6B8 = 7)
    MenuEntry --> MainMenu: next frame (state 1)

    MainMenu --> Gameplay: "Continue" (game running)
    MainMenu --> SaveGame: "Save"
    MainMenu --> LoadGame: "Load"
    MainMenu --> NewHero: "New Game"
    MainMenu --> Options: "Options"
    MainMenu --> [*]: "Exit" (quit flag)

    SaveGame --> Gameplay: slot clicked, snapshot written
    LoadGame --> Gameplay: slot clicked, snapshot loaded
    LoadGame --> MenuEntry: open/cancel fail (page 4)
    NewHero --> NewGameLoad: confirm

    Gameplay --> MenuEntry: ESC, page 0
    Gameplay --> IntroMovie: leader game-over flag\n(GAMEOVER.FLM then page 7)
    Fatal --> [*]: quit

    MenuEntry --> MainMenu: state 7 sets state 1 in the same frame
```

## 4. Per-frame sequence (`FUN_00436214`)

One invocation per `WM_USER 0x400` (60 ms budget). Order:

1. **Consume exactly one input-ring event** (`ev[16] @ 0x81C154`,
   `flags[16] @ 0x81CF70`, indices `0x81D668/0x81D66C`).
   - Shift/Ctrl down/up → modifier levels `_81D624` / `_81D5E4`.
   - ESC is consumed here and runs the ESC screen switch (below).
   - Any other key-down leaves a “leftover VK” for gameplay.
2. **Mouse button edge latch** → `_DAT_0081D69C` (press edges).
3. If the accumulated mouse delta is non-zero, integrate it
   (`FUN_00428C00`) into the clamped cursor `_81D5C0/_81D5C4`.
4. **Dispatch on `_81D5AC`** (cases below).
5. Return; WndProc then calls `FUN_0042A470` to present the frame and draw the

#### `drawer_konung_general_346baf77f7316e86c2828da7`

- **wing:** konung
- **room:** general
- **source_file:** C:\Users\z10ba\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\konung-docs-v22\state-machine\KONUNG_STATE_MACHINE.md
- **filed_at:** 2026-10-21T21:29:16.991918
- **added_by:** opencode
- **hall:** creative
- **chunk_index:** 8
- **chunk_total:** 40

5AC`** (cases below).
5. Return; WndProc then calls `FUN_0042A470` to present the frame and draw the
   software cursor.

### 4.1 Gameplay case `0` (`0x43653F`)

1. If `leader[+4] & 0x80` (leader death/final flag) → set menu page `7`,
   `_81D5F8 = 0`, play `GAMEOVER.FLM` via `FUN_0042ADE4`; the movie-end code
   sets `_81D5AC = _81D6B8 = 7`.
2. Camera follow/scroll `FUN_004353B0` (follow target `_DAT_0081D564`, 0 = free
   camera); interface timer `_DAT_0081D710`.
3. If a portal/transition pending (`_DAT_0081D6D8 < 0`), either post/load the
   destination (`FUN_0043ADF0(0)`) or handle the special “negative location”
   case; camera reset + world redraw.
4. `_DAT_0081D640++` (world tick).
5. World update sequence:
   `FUN_00420F30` → `FUN_00421B50` (group/actor dispatcher) →

#### `drawer_konung_general_900b7eb9000dabb78e44a093`

- **wing:** konung
- **room:** general
- **source_file:** C:\Users\z10ba\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\konung-docs-v22\state-machine\KONUNG_STATE_MACHINE.md
- **filed_at:** 2026-10-21T21:29:16.992925
- **added_by:** opencode
- **hall:** creative
- **chunk_index:** 9
- **chunk_total:** 40

world tick).
5. World update sequence:
   `FUN_00420F30` → `FUN_00421B50` (group/actor dispatcher) →
   `FUN_00424520` (interface update) → `FUN_0041D048` (death/action pass).
6. **Speed loop:** repeat steps 4–5 `_DAT_0081D634` extra times.
   `_DAT_0081D634` is therefore the **game-speed step count** (`0..2`), changed
   by numpad `*`/`+` (increment, cap 2) and `-` (decrement, floor 0) in
   `FUN_00435B0C`. (This resolves the former “UNKNOWN” meaning of `_81D634`.)
7. Camera pan/audio (`FUN_00428AD8`, `FUN_0043BEBC`), modal flags:
   - `_81D61C & 4` → redraw panel `FUN_0042E520`;
   - `_81D61C & 8` → reopen inventory detail `FUN_00430D6C(0)`.
8. Input block, gated by `_81D61C & 1` and not game-over:
   - leftover key → `FUN_00435B0C(vk)`;
   - mouse/UI → `FUN_0042260C()`.

   - leftover key → `FUN_00435B0C(vk)`;

   The two call sites are `0x4367E5` and `0x43680C`.

### 4.2 Main menu case `1` (`0x43681E`)

1. Hit-test the cursor against the menu item table `0x45B2B4`
   (`FUN_00438798`).
2. `_81D5F8` (game running) changes which items are valid.
3. Play sound `FUN_0042A13C(4,0,0,0)` on click.
4. Dispatch the item index through the table at `0x436CEC`:

### 4.3 New Hero case `2` (`0x436D22`)

- Hit-test `0x4604DC`; left click selects a control.
- Updates the leader template (`0x81D56C`): portrait/palette, class/archetype,
  base/current stats, derived max load/party, equipment/action fields.
- Render helpers `FUN_0042EA6C` (paper doll/character) and `FUN_0042F578`.

### 4.4 Load case `4` (`0x437802`)

- Draw 10 slots (`FUN_004304F4(0)`), selected slot `_DAT_0081D678`.
- Left click on a valid slot → `FUN_004246C0()`.
- Success → `_81D5AC = 0` (plus portal/camera refresh); failure → `_81D5AC = 7`,
  `_81D6B8 = 4`.

### 4.5 Save case `5` (`0x437890`)

- Draw 10 slots (`FUN_004304F4(1)`); the selected slot shows the editable name
  buffer `0x81CE34`.
- Left click on a valid slot → copy `0x81CE34` to `KONUNG.SAV[slot]`,
  `FUN_00424B3C()` (write `KONUNG.SAV` + `KONUNG.SA<slot>`), then
  `_81D5AC = 0`, refresh and close the interface.

- `_81D5AC = 1` immediately; clear mouse delta; close the interface.

### 4.8 New-game load `8` (`0x437D81`)

1. Clear interface/selection/camera/input state.
2. Copy the 0x100-byte leader template from `0x81D56C` to `0x81D45C`.
3. `FUN_0043A75C()` (FLAMES.RES) and `FUN_0043A870()` (GAME_x + QUEST_R.RES).
4. Copy the creation-screen values back into the live leader
   (`0x81D4xx → 0x81D56C`).
5. Derive the party/group record `_81D55C`, group activity byte
   (`+0x1B = leader[+0xD6]*3 + 1`), and current location id `_81D6C4`.
6. `FUN_0043ADF0(0)` — **new-game map load mode**.
   Failure → message (`0x450646`) and abort.
7. Center camera, `_81D5AC = 0`, render world, close interface.

### 8.1 World / map load

`FUN_0043ADF0(mode)` loads `LIGHTS.<loc>` and `KONUNG.<loc>`:

- terrain `0x6400` → `0x617FD8`;
- collision `0xC800` → `0x4FAEB0`, converted to the runtime `u16` grid
  `0x4EE6B0`;
- GRAPH placements `0x3E80` → `0x807250`;
- prop records `0x8CA0` → `0x80BD50`;
- group/entity activation, settlement prop linkage, type table `0x81B954`,
  world extent scan.

`mode 0` (new game) additionally places the population, clears object-group
activity, and rebuilds WorldObjects/ground markers. `mode 1` (load) keeps the
snapshot versions. Details are in `KONUNG_SAVE_LOAD.md` §4.4.

### 8.2 Population / AI

- Per-frame dispatcher `FUN_00421B50` selects by group class:
  `&0x40` → `FUN_004123BC` (NPC/monster action machine → `FUN_00412E00`);
  `&0x80` → `FUN_00415FC8` → `FUN_004168A4` (citizen waypoint/wander);
  neither → `FUN_0041ECC8` (party/leader path).
- Object/enemy groups idle while `Group+0x1D == 0`; mode 0 clears it.
- `FUN_00420A8C` is population growth, gated by active group class
  `0x41..0x50` and `Group+0x1C < Group+0x18`.

### 8.3 Actor action / movement

  `Entity+0x03` (`0x04` battle stance, `0x40` arrival/occupy, `0x80` graphics
  rebuild).
- Movement: click stores destination; next tick `FUN_0041ECC8` →
  `FUN_0043DA1F` (route over the `320×160` collision grid) →
  `FUN_0041435C` (step/animation commit).
- Idle/walk/attack/death rows and animation tables are documented in
  `AGENTS.md` / `KONUNG_SOURCE_OF_TRUTH.md` §16–§17.

### 8.5 Items / inventory / character screen

- Item instance table in the loaded `GAME_x`, templates/names from the executable.
- Equipment/pickup handlers `FUN_0041E450`, `FUN_0041E6BC`, `FUN_0041E8A8`.
- Character screen entered by state `0` + interface mode `3`
  (`FUN_00424330` → `FUN_0042CBD0(2)`), drawn by `FUN_0042CBD0`.
- World map / travel mode `5` (`FUN_0042CBD0(4)`): `FUN_00428390` draws the
  discovered-location grid; clicking a discovered location returns command
  `index<<8 | 0x103`. The 56-byte flag array `0x81CF80` is the discovered
  location mask; `0x45AE30` marks reserved/unrevealable entries.
- Equipment/detail mode `7` (`FUN_00430D6C`); trade/loot is the quest opcode
  `0x113` path, not mode 5.

### 8.6 Dialogue / quest

- Right-click on a human NPC with `Entity+0xD0 != 0xFF` →
  `FUN_00435290` + `FUN_0042CBD0(0)` (interface mode 1).
- Condition/action programs are executed by `FUN_00434FE8`/`FUN_00434BB8`;
  recruit `0x123`, dismiss `0x14C`, trade/loot `0x113`.
- Full trace: `DIALOG_SYSTEM.md`, `QUEST_OPCODES.md`.

- `FUN_0042A13C(id,x,y,z)` plays a preloaded clip.
- Sound ids and asset format: `SOUND_SYSTEM.md`.

### 8.8 Movies / game over

- `FUN_0042ADE4(name)` builds `<CD-root>VIDEO\<name>`, calls
  `PlayAVI.dll::playAVIFile`, and on completion restores the window and sets
  `_81D5AC = _81D6B8`.
- `WIN.FLM`, `GAMEOVER.FLM`, `POSTER.FLM` are referenced at `0x450004`,
  `0x45061C`, `0x450655`; `DEFEAT<location>.FLM` is built at runtime from
  `"DEFEAT"` @ `0x4501B0` + location + `".FLM"` @ `0x4501B7`.
- **Victory:** the world-object pickup path (`0x41115B`) sets menu page `7`
  and plays `WIN.FLM`.
- **Defeat:** the path at `0x41D6C3` sets `_81D6B8 = 0` and plays
  `DEFEAT<location>.FLM`; because the movie-end code does `_81D5AC = _81D6B8`,
  it returns to **state `0` (gameplay/respawn)**, not the menu.

  it returns to **state `0` (gameplay/respawn)**, not the menu.
- During movie playback `_DAT_0081D5F4 = 1`; the main loop uses blocking
  `GetMessage` and WndProc `WM_USER+0x0D` drives the movie pump.

### 8.9 Projectile / effect pool (`0x80B0D0`, `_DAT_0081D6A0`)

Resolved in the follow-up pass:

- `0x80B0D0` is a **100 × 0x20 effect/projectile pool**; `_DAT_0081D6A0` is the
  active count (`0..100`). It is saved/restored in `KONUNG.SA0..9`.
- `FUN_0041BF44(entity)` allocates an effect at
  `0x80B0D0 + _DAT_0081D6A0*0x20`, projects the entity's destination
  (`Entity+0x0C/+0x0E`) and initialises position, velocity, sprite/colour,
  lifetime (`+0x1D`), entity index and damage/speed fields.

#### `drawer_konung_general_7d7705bdafc16be55c514066`

- **wing:** konung

lises position, velocity, sprite/colour,
  lifetime (`+0x1D`), entity index and damage/speed fields.
- `FUN_00420F30` is the per-frame pool update: decrements lifetime, moves by
  velocity (`+0x00/+0x04` floats), checks the collision grid and nearby
  entities, applies damage/death (`FUN_0041435C` action 2/3/8/9), and removes
  expired entries by shifting the pool.
- The action machine spawns a projectile in action row `4` and consumes one
  ammo charge from the item table (`0x659B10`); row `5` is the melee branch.

### 8.10 World map / travel screen (interface mode 5)

Resolved in the follow-up pass:

- `_DAT_0081D610 == 5` is the **world map**, not inventory/trade. When
  `_DAT_0081D61C & 2` is set, `FUN_0042A470` clears the bit and calls
  `FUN_00428390`, which draws the 56-location map grid using the pair table
  `0x450EBC` and the per-resolution panel rectangle `0x462BEA`.
- `0x81CF80` is a **56-byte discovered-location mask**; `0x45AE30` marks the
  reserved/unrevealable entries (last 14 bytes are `1`).
- Clicking a discovered location returns `(index << 8) | 0x103` from the mouse
  command compiler; the left-button dispatcher uses the index as the travel
  destination.
- The mask is saved/restored in `KONUNG.SA0..9` (`0x81CF80`, 56 bytes).

### 8.11 Camera follow (`_DAT_0081D564`)

Resolved: `_DAT_0081D564` is the **camera follow target entity pointer**
(`0` = free camera).

- Ctrl+S in `FUN_00435B0C` toggles it between `0` and the leader pointer.
- `FUN_004353B0` is the per-frame follow/scroll routine: it keeps the target's
  projected position inside a dead zone and clamps the camera to the map
  extents; it also handles edge scrolling when the cursor is at the viewport
  border.
- The asset loader initialises it to the leader (`0x4395D3`).

## 9. Invariants

- Only **one** top-level state is active per frame.
- Only **one** keyboard event is consumed per frame; ESC is consumed before
  the gameplay dispatcher.
- Mouse state is sampled (not queued); a press is latched into
  `_DAT_0081D69C` and cleared when consumed.
- Gameplay ticks are 60 ms each; `_DAT_0081D634` adds 0–2 extra simulation
  steps per rendered frame.
- Save/load are **full-state snapshots** of the entity, item, world-object,
  marker, settlement and quest tables; load mode deliberately does not rebuild

# Konung sound events — implemented placement (2026-10-24)

Source: all 22 direct `E8` calls to `FUN_0042A13C` in `konung.res` + SOUNDS.RES occupancy.

## Verified id table

- UI/menu/inventory click `4`: `0x43687e` main menu, `0x435dca` M/map, `0x422914` HUD map, `0x4242e6` quest log, `0x424362` character screen, `0x42146c` engage, `0x421568` stance, `0x4216f0` weapon cycle, `0x422480` halt, `0x41e8a8` lift/put-down.
- Item use `9` (`0x41e0e9`); class-11 map/scroll item `10` (`0x414de4` cases 0x14/0x18 and common tail).
- Equip main slot `11`, or `1` when incoming/displaced item class == 2 (`0x41e680`); additional slot `0` (`0x41e872`).
- Facing dodge `6` (`0x421e1c`; `FUN_0041c3cc` returns -2).
- Dialogue open voice `0x0C..0x13` (`0x43538f`).
- Human action table `0x4146e8`: death 34; id
le 35 (roll<95); attack 36; walk 7 (leader only); ranged 3; melee 2, or 8 when Entity+0x12==2.
- Monster action table `0x414480`: bank base `arch*8-0x1D0`; +0 melee, +1 ranged, +2 death, +3 idle/walk (idle roll<95, walk roll<75), +4 attack. `0x412abc` monster ranged uses bank+1 (clips absent).
- Ambience `0x100 + (loc-1)*8 + rand()%8`, 2% per gameplay frame (`0x4365d4`).

## Archive/data facts

- SOUNDS.RES: 391 occupied clips. 387 referenced by these paths; 42/44/50/52 are unused palette-1/2 human death/attack variants (all shipped humans are palette 0).
- 70 referenced ids have no clip (e.g. human walk 7, idle 35); original checks size and skips.

## Remake implementation

- `web/src/sound/events.rs`: constants + human/monster/equip/ambient tables + tests.
- `web/src/app/sound.rs`: `play
_actor_action`, `play_melee_impact`, `play_dodge`, `tick_ambient_sound`.
- Queue is one frame: cleared at start of `PlayableApp::step` / `Frontend::step`; shell drains before step. `sound_count`/`sound_id` follow active frontend/game.
- Chance rolls use dedicated `sound_rng` (seed 0x534F554E), not `combat_rng`, so combat determinism is unchanged.
- Hooks: HUD weapon/stance/halt/engage/character; frontend main menu click; map item 10; equip 11/1/0; attack/ranged action start; melee impact; dodge; death; ambient.
- Deferred: positional pan/volume and 19050/25050 pitch variants (ids-only pass).

Verification: cargo check --all-targets; cargo test (368 tests); smoke/combat/ranged/trade/save/frontend/pack smokes all pass; wasm+pack rebuilt.

CORRECTION to §20 (2026-11-06; konung.res sha256 d698c4f231489a372cc25212ab2819720a36d7ce5307a045db684135cf679810): item weight is SIGNED.

The selector tables (FUN_0042ba40 base) store weight = i16@+0x00. Both readers sign-extend it:
- FUN_0041B0C4: single instance weight, loads the dword at base + subtype*stride and uses >> 0x10 (arithmetic).
- FUN_0041B7E4: total carried weight over 6 equipment + 5 additional + 32 inventory slots + the +0xCC ammo field; same sign-extended sum, returns int, no clamp.
Class 12 (ammo) multiplies the signed weight by the stack quantity.

The only negative template weight in the shipped data is class 11 subtype 11 «Сын Луны»: 0xD8F0 = -10000 (display -10.0 kg). Carrying it lowers the total carried weight by 10 kg. It has no FUN_00414DE4 case and is never con
sumed.

Supersedes the "weight = u16@+0x00" wording in the §20 chunk of KONUNG_SOURCE_OF_TRUTH.md.
Remake: web/src/item.rs ItemInfo.weight: i32; inventory::item_weight/carried_weight/capacity: i32; pickup/trade/character-screen comparisons signed.

# Konung CD launcher — branch and gate catalogue

Target: `konung.exe` from the original CD (`originalCD\konung.exe`),
`sha256 178dd38422a5d9826c1871fdb9ef2dd72fc2ca5b5a9cf6bf7344c65d767fc65e`.

This document summarises `evidence/konung_exe_gates.csv` and calls out the
gates that select launcher behaviour. Addresses are `konung.exe`-specific.

## 1. Coverage

| item | count |
|---|---|
| conditional branches | **10 828** |
| indirect jump tables | **13** |
| functions containing gates | 1 100+ |
| reachable procedures | 1 177 |

The high count is normal for a Delphi/VCL application: most branches are in the
VCL runtime (string handling, streaming, controls, graphics). The
application-specific gates are concentrated in `0x45C000–0x461800`.

eaming, controls, graphics). The
application-specific gates are concentrated in `0x45C000–0x461800`.

> Caveat: Delphi embeds data, method tables and bitmap blobs inside CODE. A
> linear scan can misread data bytes as instructions, so a few `globals` values
> in the CSV are ASCII fragments (`"Form"`, `"Shif"`, …). The app-region rows
> and the hand-decoded gates below are reliable.

## 3. `BGameClick` dispatcher (`0x4601C8`)

The handler is a sender-pointer comparison chain. Each `cmp esi,[ebx+off]`
selects one button; `jne` advances to the next.

| address | compare | target on match | action |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x4601E3` | `[ebx+0x2C8]` | `0x4601E3` body | hidden `KONUNG` secret |
| `0x4602F0` | `[ebx+0x2F4]` | `0x4602F0` body | **BGame** |
| `0x460355` | `[ebx+0x2CC]` | `0x460355` body | **BSetup** |
| `0x4603D4` | `[ebx+0x2D0]` | `0x4603D4` body | **BIndeo** |
| `0x460460` | `[ebx+0x2D4]` | `0x460460` body | **BDirectX** |
| `0x4604EC` | `[ebx+0x2D8]` | `0x4604EC` body | **BMediaX** |
| `0x460578` | `[ebx+0x2DC]` | `0x460578` body | **BWWW** |

Each branch then tests file existence (`FUN_00408004`) before launching; a

```text

## 7. How to read a gate row

```text
address=0x4601E3 function=0x4601C8 mnemonic=jne target=0x4602F0
flag_source=0x4601E3 flag_mnemonic=cmp flag_operands=esi, dword ptr [ebx + 0x2C8]
globals=0x... 
```

This means: if the clicked component is **not** `[ebx+0x2C8]`, continue to the
BGame comparison at `0x4602F0`.

# Konung original CD — layout, payload and music

CD root: `originalCD\` (copied from the original disc).

## 1. Root

| item | size | role |
|---|---|---|
| `Autorun.inf` | 53 | `open = Konung.exe`, icon `Konung.exe,0` |
| `konung.exe` | 482 304 | Delphi autorun **launcher** |
| `Konung.dat` | 217 636 | launcher UI archive (streaming ZIP: bitmaps + `RUN.INI`) |
| `readme.txt` | 2 159 | CP1251 system requirements/controls |
| `inst\` | — | installer + payload |
| `Konung\` | — | CD-side game data and videos |
| `Demo\` | — | 1C demo launcher and demo payloads |

`Autorun.inf`:

```ini
[autorun]
open = Konung.exe
icon = Konung.exe,0
```

bytes**; compressed file:

## 5. Music / CD audio

The CD is a mixed-mode disc: track 1 is the data track, and tracks **2–11**
are **Red Book CD-DA audio tracks**. They are not files in the ISO9660
filesystem, so Explorer does not show them (and a mounted ISO may expose only
the data track). The repository contains the extracted rips:

```
music\track02.ogg   2 159 474
music\track03.ogg   2 139 986
music\track04.ogg   2 095 120
music\track05.ogg   2 084 081
music\track06.ogg   2 170 775
music\track07.ogg   2 344 696
music\track08.ogg   1 891 264
music\track09.ogg   1 933 650
music\track10.ogg   2 138 386
music\track11.ogg   2 474 209
```

#### `drawer_konung_konung-exe_ca876fb1d86da40c22e8d008`

- **wing:** konung
- **room:** konung-exe
- **source_file:** C:\Users\z10ba\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\konung_exe_palace\konung-exe\KONUNG_EXE_CD_LAYOUT.md
- **filed_at:** 2026-10-22T19:14:09.085341
- **added_by:** opencode
- **hall:** creative
- **chunk_index:** 6
- **chunk_total:** 9

91 264
music\track09.ogg   1 933 650
music\track10.ogg   2 138 386
music\track11.ogg   2 474 209
```

- 10 tracks, matching audio tracks 2–11.
- The launcher does not play them; the single-player `konung.res` uses MCI CD
  audio (`winmm.dll::mciSendCommandA`).
- The installed game's `SOUNDS.RES`/`Sounds_.res` is a separate archive of
  digital sound effects/voices, not the music.

## 6. Streaming ZIP note

`Konung.dat`, `Install.dat` and `setup.dat` all start with `PK\x03\x04` and
contain local file headers, but they have **no End-of-Central-Directory
record** (`PK\x05\x06` absent) and only a partial/central-directory-like tail.
They are written by a streaming ZIP library (the Delphi `VCLUnZip`/`kpZipObj`
units) and must be read sequentially, not with a standard ZIP reader that
requires the central directory.

This is why the payloads are named `.dat` rather than `.zip` and why a normal
unzip tool reports "not a valid zip file".

## 7. Verified vs unresolved

**Verified**

- CD root, `Autorun.inf`, `readme.txt`, launcher, `inst`, `Konung`, `Demo`.
- `setup.dat` = 119-entry streaming ZIP with the full game payload.
- `Konung.dat` = 12-entry launcher UI ZIP with `RUN.INI`.
- `Install.dat` = 11-entry installer UI ZIP.
- Audio tracks 2–11 correspond to the 10 `music/track*.ogg` rips.

**Unresolved**

1. Exact CD-DA track lengths/ISRCs (not read from the physical disc here).
2. The 22-byte `GRAPH.RES` difference between CD and install payload.
3. Whether the original CD contains additional hidden/system files outside the
   ISO9660 data track.

## 4. How to use the CSV

- `role` is a region/reachability classification, not a semantic audit.
- `app-raw` rows are the hand-written Delphi methods in
  `0x45C000–0x461800`; `label` is the recovered method/role.
- `external` rows are the 402 imported functions.
- The gate CSV is a mechanical branch scan. Delphi embeds data and jump tables
  in CODE; a small number of `globals` values are data bytes misread as
  addresses (e.g. ASCII `"Form"`, `"Shif"`). Treat the app-region gates
  (`0x45C000–0x461800`) as the reliable behavioural set.

# Konung CD installer, uninstaller and payload

Targets on the original CD (`originalCD\`):

| file | size | SHA-256 |
|---|---|---|
| `konung.exe` | 482 304 | `178dd38422a5d9826c1871fdb9ef2dd72fc2ca5b5a9cf6bf7344c65d767fc65e` |
| `inst\Install.exe` | 562 688 | `3a7f3fd23e9975b280b48755a0111d784f5401475681c267f79cc7b143bb5dd6` |
| `Uninstall.exe` (payload / install root) | 432 128 | `581848ff91f81ed65c9d1fe189d1ace9eb36a9206ebb1af146bc9c09f3606d1e` |
| `Demo\demo.exe` | 435 712 | `bf170dc86270e2873c7aae1b32587c47559df22f4251bb1f2b0b3e62db7246eb` |

`konung.exe` is the **launcher**; `Install.exe` is the **installer**;
`Uninstall.exe` is copied into the install root by the installer. All three are
Borland Delphi applications and share the VCL/`TBo*`/`VCLUnZip` framework.

## 1. Installer entry points and UI data

`inst\Install.exe` imports the same VCL/registry/shell units and contains the
literals:

- `Install.ini`, `ListFile.ini`, `\Install.dat`, `\Install.zip`,

installer image list/bitmap; `Install.str` is a Delphi `TStringsCollection`
with the finish/parameter/readme memo texts.

Shortcut targets point to `<CDROM>\Konung.exe`, not to the installed game:
the shortcut starts the **launcher**, which then finds the installed
`Root\Konung.res` through the registry. This is why the launcher must remain on
the CD (or be copied alongside) for the shortcut to work.

## 4. Install flow

1. The launcher's **Установка игры** button runs `<CD>\inst\Install.exe`.
2. `Install.exe` reads `Install.ini` (UI/captions/shortcuts/registry) and
   `ListFile.ini` (file list).
3. It opens `Setup.dat` (or `Setup.zip`) and `Install.dat` (or `Install.zip`)
   through `VCLUnZip`/`kpZipObj`.
4. It asks for the destination folder (default `1C\Konung` under Program Files),
   checks free space, and extracts the 119 files with a progress bar
   (`Install.img`/`00..07.bmp`, `Copy_to` status).
5. It copies `Uninstall.exe`, `Uninstall.ini` and `ListFile.ini` into the
   install root.
6. It writes the registry:
   - `HKLM\Software\1C\Konung` with `Root` (install dir), `Version` (`1.0`),

#### `drawer_konung_konung-exe_b291e2e23e01cffd934171ce`

- **wing:** konung

- **added_by:** opencode

- **chunk_index:** 7
- **chunk_total:** 14

scriptor entries (`Objects.res`, `konung.res`, flags `0x0002`)
still parse from their local headers.

It writes the registry:
   - `HKLM\Software\1C\Konung` with `Root` (install dir), `Version` (`1.0`),
     and the uninstall data;
   - `HKLM\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall\Konung: Legend of
     the North` with `UninstallString`, `DisplayName`, etc.
7. It creates the shortcuts from `[Shortcut]`:
   - `<Desktop>\Князь.lnk` and `<GROUP>\Князь.lnk` → `<CDROM>\Konung.exe`;
   - `<GROUP>\Удалить.lnk` → `<ROOT>\Uninstall.exe`.
8. Finish page (`Install.str`: "Установка игры ... успешно завершена").

The launcher's installed check reads the same `Root`/`Version` values and
requires `Root\Client.exe` to exist. The game itself is launched as
`Root\Konung.res`.

[Shortcut]

- It removes the files listed by `ListFile.ini` from the install root.
- It asks whether to delete the save files (`konung.sa*`, `konung.cfg`,
  `client.log`, `clans.cfg`); declining preserves them.
- It removes the shortcuts and the uninstall registry key.

## 6. Demo launcher

`Demo\demo.exe` is a generic 1C demo installer/launcher. It bundles zlib
(`inflate 1.0.4`) and is not part of the Konung install; the CD's
`Demo\allods3\DEMO.AVI` and `Demo\WAR` are separate demo payloads. The Konung
launcher's **Демо** button runs `Demo\Demo.exe`.

# Konung CD launcher — button dispatch and actions

Target: `konung.exe` from the original CD (`originalCD\konung.exe`),
`sha256 178dd38422a5d9826c1871fdb9ef2dd72fc2ca5b5a9cf6bf7344c65d767fc65e`.
All addresses are `konung.exe`-specific.

This document decodes `BGameClick` (`0x4601C8`), the single `OnClick` handler
shared by all ten bitmap buttons on `TFormStart`. The handler compares the
`Sender` (Delphi `Self`/`Sender` convention: `EAX = Self`, `EDX = Sender`) with
the form's component fields and dispatches to the corresponding action.

## 1. Handler shape

`BGameClick` is in the raw code region (Ghidra did not define a function at
`0x4601C8`). It was decoded from bytes; the method-table entry is
`0x45FAFA → 0x4601C8`.

#### `drawer_konung_konung-exe_86e77b459d893fbfe89908fa`

- **wing:** konung
- **room:** konung-exe
- **source_file:** C:\Users\z10ba\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\konung_exe_palace\konung-exe\KONUNG_EXE_LAUNCHER.md
- **filed_at:** 2026-10-22T19:14:10.150605
- **added_by:** opencode
- **hall:** technical
- **chunk_index:** 1
- **chunk_total:** 12

function at
`0x4601C8`). It was decoded from bytes; the method-table entry is
`0x45FAFA → 0x4601C8`.

```text
push ebp; mov ebp, esp; ...            # 0x4601C8
...
cmp esi, [ebx + 0x2C8]                 # hidden "KONUNG" branch
jne 0x4602F0
...
cmp esi, [ebx + 0x2F4]                 # BGame
cmp esi, [ebx + 0x2CC]                 # BSetup
cmp esi, [ebx + 0x2D0]                 # BIndeo
cmp esi, [ebx + 0x2D4]                 # BDirectX
cmp esi, [ebx + 0x2D8]                 # BMediaX
cmp esi, [ebx + 0x2DC]                 # BWWW
cmp esi, [ebx + 0x2E0]                 # BAuthor
cmp esi, [ebx + 0x2E4]                 # BDemo
cmp esi, [ebx + 0x2E8]                 # BInfo
```

| `+0x2D8` | `BMediaX` | **Установка Microsoft DirectX Media 6.0** | runs `<CD>\\Inst\\dxmed60.exe` via `TBoRun` |
| `+0x2DC` | `BWWW` | **WWW** | `ShellExecuteA("Open", <RUN.INI Install.URL>)` |

| `+0x2E4` | `BDemo` | **Демо** | `WinExec("<CD>\\Demo\\Demo.exe")` |

`ebx` is the form instance; `esi` is the clicked component pointer. The chain
ends at `0x460753`; there is no default action.

## 2. Button table

| form field | button | UI label (observed) | action |

| `+0x2CC` | `BSetup` | **Установка игры** | `WinExec("<CD>\\inst\\Install.exe")` |
| `+0x2D0` | `BIndeo` | **Установка Intel Indeo 5.06** | runs `<CD>\\Inst\\indeo506.exe` via `TBoRun` |
| `+0x2D4` | `BDirectX` | **Установка Microsoft DirectX 6.0** | runs `<CD>\\Inst\\dx6eng.exe` via `TBoRun` |

`BUninst` exists in the DFM and has `OnClick = BGameClick`, but its field offset
and dispatch branch were not located; it is not visible in the observed
(non-installed) launcher. Uninstallation is provided by the installed
`Uninstall.exe` and its shortcut.

## 3. Game button (`+0x2F4`, `0x4602F0`)

`FUN_0045FE84` (`0x45FE84`,
`ev_c519fc34140fc82650fcd80d71d52708624d450a7cc6c0542ba9f61969150b5a`)
checks whether the game is installed:

```c
// read HKLM\Software\1C\Konung
RegQueryValueExA(hkey, "Version", ..., &version);
RegQueryValueExA(hkey, "Root",    ..., &root);
if (file_exists(root + "\\Client.exe")) installed = true;
```

#### `drawer_konung_konung-exe_ac1545508b04d38381253df2`

- **wing:** konung
- **room:** konung-exe
- **source_file:** C:\Users\z10ba\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\konung_exe_palace\konung-exe\KONUNG_EXE_LAUNCHER.md
- **filed_at:** 2026-10-22T19:14:10.153668
- **added_by:** opencode
- **hall:** creative
- **chunk_index:** 5
- **chunk_total:** 12

ValueExA(hkey, "Root",    ..., &root);
if (file_exists(root + "\\Client.exe")) installed = true;
```

- `RegRoot` comes from `RUN.INI [Install] RegRoot`
  (`\Software\1C\Konung`).
- `Version` must match `RUN.INI [Install] Version` (`1.0`) for the first check.
- The **installation marker** is `Root\Client.exe`.
- The game executable that the launcher runs is **`Root\Konung.res`** (the
  single-player PE), not `Client.exe`:

```c
WinExec(root + "\\Konung.res", SW_SHOW);
```

If the game is not installed, the handler shows the `game_not_set` hint
(`Игра не установлена`); no launch occurs.

`CoCreateInstance`. The hints in `FUN_00460024` reflect these results.

## 4. Install button (`+0x2CC`, `0x460355`)

Builds `<CD>\\inst\\Install.exe`, checks it exists, and runs it with `WinExec`.
If missing, it displays `File <path> not found` (the `0x4607B4` / `0x4607C4`
strings). The installer is a separate Delphi program (`inst\Install.exe`,
562 688 bytes) documented in `KONUNG_EXE_INSTALLER.md`.

## 5. DirectX / Indeo / Media buttons (`+0x2D0/+0x2D4/+0x2D8`)

All three use the same pattern:

```c
path = "<CD>\\Inst\\<installer>.exe";
if (!file_exists(path)) { show "File <path> not found"; }
else {
    run_helper(path);          // 0x45D004 / 0x45D050 (TBoRun)
}
```

The URL is `RUN.INI [Install] URL = http://www.1c.ru/konung/` (global at

## 7. Authors (`+0x2E0`, `0x4605A5`)

1. `FUN_0045FC0C` verifies DirectShow availability.
2. Builds `<CD>\\Konung\\Video\\credits.flm` (`0x460834`).
3. Shows the `TFAVI` form and plays the FLIC/AVI credits through the
   `MP_AviM`/`DShow` units.
4. Repositions the form around the video window (`0x45F118`/`0x45F120`).

The `TFAVI` DFM resource contains `TMP_AviM` with `OnEnd = AviEnd`,
`OnKeyDown`, `OnMouseUp`; the video is `credits.flm` (13 822 842 bytes on the
CD).

## 8. Demo (`+0x2E4`, `0x46066F`)

Builds `<CD>\\Demo\\Demo.exe` and runs it with `WinExec`. `Demo.exe`
(435 712 bytes) is a separate 1C demo launcher; the CD also contains
`Demo\allods3\DEMO.AVI` and the `Demo\WAR` installer.

## 9. Info (`+0x2E8`, `0x4606EE`)

Builds `<CD>\\Readme.txt` and opens it with
`ShellExecuteA(hwnd, "Open", path, ...)`. The CD's `readme.txt` is a CP1251
document with system requirements and controls.

## 10. Hidden `KONUNG` branch (`+0x2C8`, `0x4601E3`)

The first branch is a secret/typed-code path:

```c
if (Sender == form->[0x2C8]) {
    s = first_char_of_input;           // 0x45CA20
    if (s != "KONUNG") { show message; return; }
    if (installed) WinExec(root + "\\Konung.res");
}
```

The exact UI trigger (the dragon/CD emblem at the bottom of the launcher) is
confirmed by the image mapping: `+0x2C8` loads `game.bmp`. The code compares a
string to `"KONUNG"`; the precise input buffer (`0x464A90`) and the
`sub al,0x40` check suggest a keyboard/typed sequence, but the full trigger is
not proven.

## 11. Runtime confirmation

The captured launcher window shows the labels in §2. The observed window class
is `Setup_Run`, title `Князь: Легенды Лесной страны`, size 506 × 389. No files
were written to the CD root during the run; the launcher only reads
`Konung.dat` and `RUN.INI`.

# Konung CD launcher — startup, Delphi form and RUN.INI

Target: `konung.exe` from the original CD (`originalCD\konung.exe`), PE32/i386,
image base `0x400000`, 482 304 bytes,
`sha256 178dd38422a5d9826c1871fdb9ef2dd72fc2ca5b5a9cf6bf7344c65d767fc65e`.

This is the **CD autorun launcher**, not the game engine. It is a Borland
Delphi application (`TPF0` / `TFormStart`, VCL, `PACKAGEINFO`). All addresses are
`konung.exe`-specific.

Companion documents: `KONUNG_EXE_LAUNCHER.md`, `KONUNG_EXE_INSTALLER.md`,
`KONUNG_EXE_CD_LAYOUT.md`, `KONUNG_EXE_FUNCTION_MAP.md`,
`KONUNG_EXE_BRANCHES.md`.

`PACKAGEINFO` lists the application units: `wkonung`, `MP_AviM`, `GMMSystem`,
`DShow`, `DSound`, `DDraw`, `VCLUnZip`, `kpZipObj`, `BoRun`, `BoFileList`,
`BoMousServ`, `BoJustOne`, `Registry`, `IniFiles`, `Reg_ini`, `WinShell`,
`ShellAPI`, `BoShlobj`, `BoVerInfo`, `XZipFunction`, `GameSetupConst`,
`ButtonM`, `BmpPanel`, `AVarKonung`, `UAvI`.

The unit list already identifies the three functional layers:

1. `VCLUnZip` + `kpZipObj` + `XZipFunction` — ZIP extraction.
2. `BoRun` + `WinShell` + `ShellAPI` + `BoShlobj` — process launch/shell.
3. `Registry` + `IniFiles` + `Reg_ini` — registry/INI configuration.

— `FUN_00460AC8` (`0x460AC8`)

`ev_17f76fa59ad71679bfdccdc2de0e2499e4bca21f35f072bbf69e3fd6693d5b5e`

```c
if (file_exists("\\konung.zip")) {
    extract("\\konung.zip");
    load_ini(form, "\\konung.zip", "run.ini");
} else if (file_exists("\\konung.dat")) {
    extract("\\konung.dat");
    load_ini(form, "\\konung.dat", "run.ini");
}
```

- The launcher prefers `konung.zip`, then `konung.dat`.
- Both are **streaming ZIP archives** (local headers, no central directory).
- `FUN_0045BCD0` is the extract/INI-load helper; the `VCLUnZip`/`kpZipObj`
  units and the `TBoRun`/`TBoFile` components implement it.
- The archive contains the launcher background/button bitmaps and `RUN.INI`.

Updates the hint labels and enables/disables the install buttons:

- `FUN_0045FB8C` — checks the **Indeo 5.0 codec** (`VIDC.IV50`); sets
  `set_yes`/`set_not`.
- `FUN_0045FDD0` — checks **DirectX** through `DSetup.dll` /
  `DirectXSetupGetVersion`; sets the DirectX hint.
- `FUN_0045FC0C` — checks **DirectX Media/DirectShow** via
  `CoCreateInstance`; sets the media hint.
- `FUN_0045FE84` — checks the **game installation** (see launcher doc).
- The game button hint becomes `run_game` when installed, `game_not_set`
  otherwise; install buttons are enabled/disabled accordingly.

# Konung CD launcher investigation index (2026-10-22)

Target: originalCD\konung.exe, PE32/i386, 482304 B,
sha256 178dd38422a5d9826c1871fdb9ef2dd72fc2ca5b5a9cf6bf7344c65d767fc65e.
Borland Delphi VCL application (TPF0/TFormStart), 2787 procedures.

Verdict: it is the CD autorun LAUNCHER/installer front end, not the game.
- Reads Konung.dat (streaming ZIP: background/button bitmaps + RUN.INI).
- Buttons: ИГРА, Установка игры, Indeo 5.06, DirectX 6.0, DirectX Media 6.0,
  WWW, Авторы, Демо, Информация (+ hidden KONUNG secret + hidden BUninst).
- ИГРА checks HKLM\Software\1C\Konung Version/Root and Root\Client.exe, then
  runs Root\Konung.res.
- Установка игры runs <CD>\inst\Install.exe.
- Installer extracts setup.dat (75,543,320 B streaming ZIP, 119 entries,
  142,835,552 B raw) into the inst
all root, writes registry Root/Version and
  the uninstall key, creates shortcuts.
- CD music = Red Book audio tracks 2..11; repo music/track02..11.ogg are rips.

Documents (room konung-exe):
KONUNG_EXE_STARTUP_FORM.md, KONUNG_EXE_LAUNCHER.md, KONUNG_EXE_INSTALLER.md,
KONUNG_EXE_CD_LAYOUT.md, KONUNG_EXE_FUNCTION_MAP.md, KONUNG_EXE_BRANCHES.md.

State machine: 9 intro → 7 menu entry → 1 main menu → {0 gameplay, 2 new hero → 8 → 0, 4 load → 0, 5 save → 0, 6 options, 3 fatal}; ESC 0→7/0→1; victory WIN.FLM → page 7; defeat DEFEAT<loc>.FLM → state 0; leader death → GAMEOVER.FLM. Package F1 (2026-10) population growth implemented in web/src/actors/growth.rs. FUN_00420A8C is called ONLY from FUN_0043ADF0(mode 0) at map entry (not per frame). grow_map visits groups 1..200 and skips the FIRST eligible class-0x41..0x50 group of the location because FUN_0043ADF0 initialises local_2c=1; every later eligible group grows while signed Group+0x02 < 0 and signed (char)+0x1C < (char)+0x18. Each created member adds the high 16 bits of dword 0x45E216+class*4 to +0x02: class 0x41..0x50 = 0x05A0,0x0B40,0x12C0,0x0E10,0x0960,0x0960,0x0E10,0x0960,0x0960,0x0960,0x0708,0x0960,0x0780,0x0E10,0x0B40,0x0E10; a still-negative accumulator is clamped to 0. FUN_0041BD3C runs every 16 logic frames: if (char)+0x18 < (char)+0x19 it decrements +0x06 and, when signed +0x06 < 0, increments +0x18 and
 adds the low 16 bits of dword 0x45E31E+(class-0x41)*4 = 0x0E10,0x1770,0x2EE0,0x2328,0x1770,0x0E10,0x1C20,0x0E10,0x12C0,0x1770,0x1518,0x1518,0x0E10,0x0E10,0x12C0,0x12C0; then while (char)+0x1C < (char)+0x18 it decrements +0x02. FUN_00420A8C averages +0x54, +0x58, +0x33, +0x34, +0x4E, +0x52, +0x5E, +0x11, +0x62; sets HP +0x5C=0x640; clears +0x66 (6x u16), +0x72 (32x u16), +0xB2 (5x u16); copies +0xD6/+0xD8 from a random member; +0x05=group index; +0x03=4; +0xD0=0xFF; +0xD4/+0xD5=0; class L additionally +0x12=rand%5+1; kind=4 for class L/F/U else 0. The constructor places the member itself and the caller places it a second time; a failed caller placement leaves the member counted but unregistered. growth_timer/growth_countdown/count_18/capacity/entity_count are saved: save v3 adds growth_tim
er+growth_countdown to GroupExtra, v1/v2 decode with zeros.

KONUNG.CFG: 28 bytes = 7 u32: +0x00 video mode (0x111=640x480, 0x114=800x600); +0x04 Прозрачность; +0x08 Смена дня и ночи; +0x0C Световые эффекты; +0x10 Музыка (_81D60C, MCI); +0x14 Тени; +0x18 Звук (_81D62C, gates FUN_0042A13C SFX). Missing → six flags 1, mode 0x111.

KONUNG.SAV: 500 B = 10×50-byte CP866 names in 0x653518; missing → "Пустая строка", _81D678=-1. Save name = location default (0x45B394+(loc-1)*0x28) + space + `Www Mmm dd hh:mm` (asctime truncated at the colon before seconds), stored from 0x81CE34.

KONUNG.SA0..SA9: 0xB07A4 B:
0x0000 _81D5EC; 0x0004 _81D604; 0x0008 _81D640 tick; 0x000C leader index; 0x0010 _81D6A0 effect count; 0x0014 _81D6A8 marker count; 0x0018 0x4B0 quest flags; 0x04C8 0xC80 0x80B0D0 effect pool (100×0x20); 0x1148 0x20000 item table; 0x21148 0x3200 markers
; 0x24348 0x1900 actor records; 0x25C48 0x7D00 WorldObjects; 0x2D948 0x7D000 Entities; 0xAA948 0x5E24 settlements; 0xB076C 0x38 0x81CF80 56 discovered map flags. End 0xB07A4.

Save FUN_00424B3C; load FUN_004246C0 (seek 0x2D948+idx*0x100 leader copy, load FLAMES/GAME_x/QUEST, read blocks, FUN_0043ADF0(1)). Slot KONUNG.SA + digit 0..9. Mode 1 does not rebuild population/WorldObjects/markers.

- writer FUN_00424B3C, reader FUN_004246C0, 0xB07A4 bytes.
- save offset 0x18 = quest B7 flags 300 x u32 (0x4B0); loaded after
  FUN_0043A870 reloads Game.N + QUEST_R.RES, then overwrites the whole B7 block.
  Slot = {u8 flags(0x80 active), u8 unknown, u16 journal line}.
- save offset 0x24348 0x1900 = Group[200] table (200 x 0x20), NOT a 400x0x10
  actor-name table. Verified from FUN_0043A870 (read 0x1900 -> 0x67C08C) and
  FUN_00432F8C (short* +0x10 => byte stride 0x20; first_entity +0, count +0x18,
  capacity +0x19, location +0x1B, entity_count +0x1C, class +0x1E).
- reader load mode 1 does not re-place population/WorldObjects/markers.

Remake implementation:
- web/src/save.rs: OPKS header + 
u16 version + raw length + bincode + deflate.
  SaveGame contains meta(profile/hero/current map), PlayerSave, groups,
  entities, settlements, settlement_trade, WorldObject sources, active markers,
  per-map PopulationBuild, party, party_selected, full quest_flags Vec<u32>,
  discovered_locations[56], world_frame, item_table (0x20000), projectiles, RNG.
- web/src/app/save.rs: capture_save / apply_save. Load restores caches directly
  (no placement/rebuild), clears transient UI/combat, centers camera.
- web/src/abi/save_api.rs: save_snapshot[_ptr/_len], load_snapshot,
  set_save_name, save_default_name[_ptr/_len], take_save/load_request.
  Fresh-runtime load calls start_game(hero, build from save) then apply_save.
- web/src/frontend: exact 0x46241C 10-slot rects (640 at +0, 800 at +0x10),
 
 slot hover/click requests, names drawn in original small font.
- web/www/app.js: openkonung.save.<slot> = JSON {v,name,created,data(base64)};
  names in openkonung.sav; processSaveRequests() each frame.
- quest_flags upgraded from Vec<u8> to Vec<u32>; QuestData::initial_flags
  returns full slots; settlement progress_14 parsed/saved; discovered mask
  marks map entry.
- tools/save_smoke.mjs: live save/load, frontend slot requests, fresh-runtime load.
  Snapshot ~35 KB compressed for a Game.1 save.

rs). PlayAVI.dll (sha256 0282620e…142c) starts ONE helper thread: FUN_004281F0 + thread-local WH_GETMESSAGE hook, CreateThread 0x405EC0 → start 0x428150, singleton. Body: loops on active video object [obj+0x48], calls frame render 0x429FE4, waits on event 100 ms (WAIT_TIMEOUT repeats).

Transitions: 9→7→1. Menu: 0 continue→0, 1 save→5, 2 load→4, 3 new game→2, 4 options→6, 5 exit. New hero confirm→8→0 (mode 0), back→7/2. Load→0 or 7/4. Save→0. Options→7/6; video fail→3. #### `drawer_konung_world-objects_9f0a7eb16a3ac016964687ef`

- **wing:** konung
- **room:** world-objects
- **filed_at:** 2026-11-06T23:09:54.635894

- **chunk_index:** 0
- **chunks:** 3

DIG SYSTEM — buried WorldObjects (2026-11-06; konung.res d698c4f231489a372cc25212ab2819720a36d7ce5307a045db684135cf679810).

Table: Game.N +0x21900, 500 x 0x40.
+0x00/+0x04 i32 activation timestamps; +0x08/+0x0A runtime-grid cell; +0x0C sprite id; +0x0E source index (0 => WIN.FLM); +0x10 i16 money (NEGATIVE = BURIED); +0x12 location (0 = permanently removed); +0x14..+0x3F 22 item-instance ids.

Every Game.0/1/2 ships 92 buried records (identical coordinates; item ids differ per profile).

Verified rules:
- FUN_0043ADF0 keeps buried records in the active super pool: the filter tests location + activation only (+0x00 >= 0, or +0x00 != +0x04, or now + +0x00 >= 0x1518). Money sign is irrelevant.
- FUN_0042532C marker pass draws only when -1 < (i16)+0x10, i.e. money >= 0. Buried records are inv
isible with or without the torch (_DAT_0081D628).
- FUN_00428740 (0x428A2B) and the hover branch 0x423CD2 also require money >= 0; the hover message is 0x45C31C «Что-то лежит на земле».
- Arrival pickup FUN_00421B50: if (cell & 0x2000) && (money >= 0 || FUN_00433A8C(0x19) != 0) call FUN_004110EC.
- FUN_00433A8C(n) scans the leader's 32 inventory slots (Entity+0x72..+0xB1) for class 0x0B and subtype n-1, returns slot+1. 0x19 => subtype 0x18 = 24 = «Лопата». Bag only; equipment/additional slots do not count.
- FUN_004110EC pays FUN_0043EAA2(money) = abs(money) and then transfers the +0x14 items with the usual weight/capacity checks.
- The copper mirror (class-11 subtype 48, FUN_00414DE4 case 0x30) negates +0x10 for every record of the CURRENT location (source table + active markers); it is c
onsumed. This is the "enlightenment" of all dig spots.
- Shovel use (case 0x18) only sets interface mode _DAT_0081D610 = 0, cursor _DAT_0081D660 = 7 and sound 10; it is not consumed. FUN_0042260C resets the cursor to 4 on the next world click. There is no per-use dig table.
- Emptied records: FUN_0041093C respawns only a sole class-9 subtype-1/2 item; everything else gets +0x12 = 0.

Quest example: «Лезвие древнего меча» (class 11 subtype 23) is buried at location 15, cell (50,49), money -100. «Рукоять древнего меча» (subtype 22) is a VISIBLE pile at location 36, cell (34,82), money 0.

Full 92-row table + remake mapping: repo DIG_SYSTEM.md.

- C2: skill books 1–5, strength 8, identify 9 (clears instance +0x0E bit 0 on owned items and raises leader identification), XP 10, wine 12, birch letter 28 (B7 slot 0 |= 0x80), torch 31 (torch_reveal until map load), charisma 36/39, relics 38 (+0xD3 = 0x46).
- C3: duplicator 7, sword parts 22/23, amulet parts 25/26/27/41/42/43, held-item-on-target routing.
- C4: resurrection 6, magnet 13, orbs 14–19/21, map orb 20, skeleton lamp 30, whistle/pipe 35/37, keys 44/52/53, mirror 48.
- D2: doll 33 writes Entity+0xD2 = 0x13 for every living actor of every other group in the current location, then consumes.
- 24 «
Лопата» arms the dig cursor and is not consumed (see the digging package).
Successful uses queue sound 10 once; subtype 0 already queues it from use_map_item.

f NPC +0xD2 is still unlocated; the write and consumption are faithful.

Digging / buried WorldObjects (2026-11-06).

The shovel is not a use effect: a buried treasure is a Game.N WorldObject record with negative +0x10 money, and the shovel is a CARRIED requirement.

Implementation:
- app/world_items.rs::rebuild_world_objects keeps buried records in active_world_objects (previously filtered out).
- app/world_draw.rs skips markers with money < 0 (FUN_0042532C).
- app/cursor.rs: ground cursor requires money >= 0; dig_armed forces frame 7.
- try_pickup_here: buried marker requires shovel_in_bag() (class 11 subtype 24 in the 32 inventory slots, FUN_00433A8C(0x19)); pays abs(money) (FUN_0043EAA2).
- app/class11.rs case 24: closes the character screen, sets dig_armed, sound 10, item NOT consumed; any world click clears it (FUN_0042260C).
- Copper mirror flips the mon
ey for the current location (already implemented), which reveals the spots and makes them pickable without the shovel.
Tests: e1_buried_spot_needs_the_shovel_to_be_collected, e1_copper_mirror_reveals_a_buried_spot, e1_shovel_arms_the_dig_cursor_and_is_not_consumed.
Data: 92 buried spots per profile; «Лезвие древнего меча» loc 15 (50,49). Doc: DIG_SYSTEM.md.

2026-11-10 — parity gap closure (konung.res 0x4104D4/0x4105A0, 0x41130C, 0x43C040/0x43D247).

REA findings (Ghidra 12.1.4, sha d698c4f2…9810):
• 0x41130C is a DETERMINISTIC unit-step DDA line-of-fire probe, not random scatter. FUN_0043EAAE = ROUND(ST0) (x87 FISTP), FUN_0043EB2E = sqrt domain check. shots = u16@0x459B84+subtype*0x40 (1 melee, 18/25 ranged), 8 samples per shot, start nudge 0x45ACD0[dir], aim y-0x1E, epsilon 0.001 at 0x450014. Used by 0x410E84 (armed target pick, gate Entity+0x12==1 + equipment[0] + ammo) and 0x41ECC8.
• 0x4104D4/0x4105A0: NPC auto-equip. class==slot for slots 0..5, ammo class 12 at Entity+0xCC, order 0,1,3,4,5,2, then Entity+0x12 = bow?1:slot0?0:2. Callers only 0x412E00 and 0x4168A4. FUN_00421AD0 = shift-down inventory removal.
• 0x43C040/0x43D247: the 'dirt
y region' system is a 600-entry per-SCANLINE run-list accumulator (DAT_004C6B50), not damage rects. 0x43C040 clips a sprite-shaped RLE into it; 0x43D247 halves pixels ((c&0xF7DF)>>1) = the actor/projectile GROUND SHADOW pass, gated by config shadows 0x81D620, inside FUN_00426384. GRAPH frame record = {u32 shadow_off, u32 sprite_off} (human +0x1728, object +0x728; data +0x4D28/+0x3228); anchor record has TWO (gx,gy) pairs: +0/+2 shadow, +4/+6 sprite. Sample: human base0 f0 43x85 sprite + 107x22 shadow at 2167; object0 237x43.
• Corrections to the earlier audit: 0x4203F4 is the TRADE confirm (already ported), 0x438DC4/0x438E24 are SOUND pan/volume (already in sound/position.rs).

Implemented: combat/los.rs (+8 tests), inventory::auto_equip_slot/refresh_equipment (+2 tests), app::refresh_npc_
equipment in combat_tick, render/spans.rs (add_mask/darken, 5 tests), res/shadows.rs (body+overlay masks, 4 tests + sweep), WorldSprite::shadow, draw_detail pass, debug_shadow_spans/debug_shadow_span, tools/shadow_smoke.mjs.
Gotcha: wasm32 usize is 32-bit — garbage u32 offsets from GRAPH wrapped and caused a bounds panic in add_mask; all mask/offset math now uses u64.
Shadow clip must be SCREEN space (0..fw-1), because add_actor_shadow's origin already includes the camera pan.
Docs: AGENTS.md (shadow + gear refresh + LOS bullets, smoke list), KONUNG_SOURCE_OF_TRUTH.md §28.9, docs/MODULES.md, PARITY_GAPS_PLAN.md §8.
State: cargo test 529 passed; check clean; wasm+pack rebuilt; smoke/shadow/mob_ai/ranged/combat/trade/save/legacy/settings/lighting/frontend/keymap/video/feedback all PASS.
Cove
rage: 268/291 faithful (92.1%), 0 partial, 17 platform, 6 absent.
